{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# Sparsity predictor architecture: bottleneck MLP + Gumbel-sigmoid/STE mask + model wrapper.\\n# Compensation network: per-layer MLP correcting nn\\n\\n\\nclass SparsityPredictor(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Rank-bottleneck MLP predicting SwiGLU sparsity masks.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, hidden_size: int, intermediate_size: int, bottleneck_size: int = 128):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.down = nn.Linear(hidden_size, bottleneck_size)\\n        self.act = nn.GELU()\\n        self.up = nn.Linear(bottleneck_size, intermediate_size)\\n\\n    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        return self.up(self.act(self.down(hidden_states)))\\n\\n\\nclass CompensationHead(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Per-layer MLP predicting the output error introduced by sparsification.\\n\\n    Takes MLP input hidden state, outputs correction added to sparse MLP output.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, hidden_size: int, bottleneck_size: int = 256):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\\n            nn.Linear(hidden_size, bottleneck_size),\\n            nn.GELU(),\\n            nn.Linear(bottleneck_size, hidden_size),\\n        )\\n\\n    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        return self.net(hidden_states)\\n\\n\\nclass CompensationNetwork(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Per-layer compensation heads correcting sparse MLP output errors.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, num_layers: int, hidden_size: int, bottleneck_size: int = 256):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.heads = nn.ModuleList([\\n            CompensationHead(hidden_size, bottleneck_size)\\n            for _ in range(num_layers)\\n        ])\\n\\n    def forward_layer(self, layer_idx: int, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        return self.heads[layer_idx](hidden_states)\\n\\n\\nclass GumbelSigmoidMask(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Differentiable binary mask via Gumbel-sigmoid relaxation.\\n\\n    Training: soft mask with Gumbel noise and temperature annealing.\\n    Inference: hard threshold (sigmoid(logits) > 0.5 <=> logits > 0).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, tau: float = 1.0, hard: bool = False):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.tau = tau\\n        self.hard = hard\\n\\n    def forward(self, logits: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        if self.training and not self.hard:\\n            u = torch.rand_like(logits).clamp(1e-6, 1 - 1e-6)\\n            gumbel_noise = -torch.log(-torch.log(u))\\n            y_soft = torch.sigmoid((logits + gumbel_noise) / self.tau)\\n            return y_soft\\n        else:\\n            return (logits > 0).to(logits.dtype)\\n\\n\\n\\nclass STEMask(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Straight-Through Estimator binary mask.\\n\\n    Forward: hard threshold (sigmoid > 0.5).\\n    Backward: gradient flows through sigmoid (vanilla STE).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def forward(self, logits: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        if self.training:\\n            sigmoid_out = torch.sigmoid(logits)\\n            hard = (sigmoid_out > 0.5).to(logits.dtype)\\n            return hard - sigmoid_out.detach() + sigmoid_out\\n        else:\\n            return (logits > 0).to(logits.dtype)\\n\\n\\n\\nclass PredictorWrapper(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Wraps a frozen LlamaForCausalLM with per-layer sparsity predictors.\\n\\n    Monkey-patches each MLP to optionally apply a predicted mask on the\\n    SwiGLU intermediate activation (act(gate_proj(x)) * up_proj(x)) before down_proj.\\n\\n    Modes:\\n        sparse_mode=True:  apply Gumbel-sigmoid mask (training/eval)\\n        sparse_mode=False: passthrough; optionally capture intermediates for BCE\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, model, bottleneck_size: int = 128):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.model = model\\n        for p in model.parameters():\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\n\\n        config = model.config\\n        num_layers = config.num_hidden_layers\\n        hidden_size = config.hidden_size\\n        intermediate_size = config.intermediate_size\\n\\n        self.predictors = nn.ModuleList([\\n            SparsityPredictor(hidden_size, intermediate_size, bottleneck_size)\\n            for _ in range(num_layers)\\n        ])\\n        self.gumbel_mask = GumbelSigmoidMask()\\n\\n        self.sparse_mode = True\\n        self.capture_intermediates = False\\n        self._layer_masks = {}\\n        self._layer_intermediates = {}\\n        self._layer_inputs = {}\\n\\n        # Compensation network (set externally, None by default)\\n        self.comp_network = None\\n        self.use_compensation = False\\n\\n        self._patch_mlps()\\n\\n    def _patch_mlps(self):\\n        for layer_idx, layer in enumerate(self.model.model.layers):\\n            self._patch_single_mlp(layer_idx, layer.mlp)\\n\\n    def _patch_single_mlp(self, layer_idx, mlp):\\n        gate_proj = mlp.gate_proj\\n        up_proj = mlp.up_proj\\n        down_proj = mlp.down_proj\\n        act_fn = mlp.act_fn\\n        wrapper = self\\n        predictor = self.predictors[layer_idx]\\n\\n        def patched_forward(x):\\n            gate = act_fn(gate_proj(x))\\n            up = up_proj(x)\\n            intermediate = gate * up\\n\\n            if wrapper.sparse_mode:\\n                logits = predictor(x)\\n                mask = wrapper.gumbel_mask(logits)\\n                wrapper._layer_masks[layer_idx] = mask\\n                result = down_proj(intermediate * mask)\\n                if wrapper.use_compensation and wrapper.comp_network is not None:\\n                    result = result + wrapper.comp_network.forward_layer(layer_idx, x)\\n                return result\\n            elif wrapper.capture_intermediates:\\n                wrapper._layer_intermediates[layer_idx] = intermediate.detach()\\n                wrapper._layer_inputs[layer_idx] = x.detach()\\n\\n            return down_proj(intermediate)\\n\\n        mlp.forward = patched_forward\\n\\n    def forward(self, input_ids, attention_mask=None, labels=None):\\n        self._layer_masks.clear()\\n        return self.model(\\n            input_ids=input_ids,\\n            attention_mask=attention_mask,\\n            labels=labels,\\n        )\\n\\n    def forward_dense(self, input_ids, attention_mask=None, capture_intermediates=False):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Forward without masks. Optionally captures per-layer intermediates & inputs.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.sparse_mode = False\\n        self.capture_intermediates = capture_intermediates\\n        self._layer_intermediates.clear()\\n        self._layer_inputs.clear()\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            outputs = self.model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\\n        self.sparse_mode = True\\n        self.capture_intermediates = False\\n        return outputs\\n\\n    def forward_sparse(self, input_ids, attention_mask=None, labels=None):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Forward with predicted sparsity masks.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.sparse_mode = True\\n        return self.forward(input_ids, attention_mask, labels)\\n\\n    def get_masks(self):\\n        return dict(self._layer_masks)\\n\\n    def get_intermediates(self):\\n        return dict(self._layer_intermediates)\\n\\n    def get_layer_inputs(self):\\n        return dict(self._layer_inputs)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 2.14}}"}

self.tau)\\n            return y_soft\\n        else:\\n            return (logits > 0).to(logits.dtype)\\n\\n\\n\\nclass STEMask(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Straight-Through Estimator binary mask.\\n\\n    Forward: hard threshold (sigmoid > 0.5).\\n    Backward: gradient flows through sigmoid (vanilla STE).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def forward(self, logits: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        if self.training:\\n            sigmoid_out = torch.sigmoid(logits)\\n            hard = (sigmoid_out > 0.5).to(logits.dtype)\\n            return hard - sigmoid_out.detach() + sigmoid_out\\n        else:\\n            return (logits > 0).to(logits.dtype)\\n\\n\\n\\nclass PredictorWrapper(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Wraps a frozen LlamaForCausalLM with per-layer sparsity predictors.\\n\\n    Monkey-patches each MLP to optionally apply a predicted mask on the\\n    SwiGLU intermediate activation (act(gate_proj(x)) * up_proj(x)) before down_proj.\\n\\n    Modes:\\n        sparse_mode=True:  apply Gumbel-sigmoid mask (training/eval)\\n        sparse_mode=False: passthrough; optionally capture intermediates for BCE\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, model, bottleneck_size: int = 128):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.model = model\\n        for p in model.parameters():\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\n\\n        config = model.config\\n        num_layers = config.num_hidden_layers\\n        hidden_size = config.hidden_size\\n        intermediate_size = config.intermediate_size\\n\\n        self.predictors = nn.ModuleList([\\n            SparsityPredictor(hidden_size, intermediate_size, bottleneck_size)\\n            for _ in range(num_layers)\\n        ])\\n        self.gumbel_mask = GumbelSigmoidMask()\\n\\n        self.sparse_mode = True\\n        self.capture_intermediates = False\\n        self._layer_masks = {}\\n        self._layer_intermediates = {}\\n        self._layer_inputs = {}\\n\\n        # Compensation network (set externally, None by default)\\n        self.comp_network = None\\n        self.use_compensation = False\\n\\n        self._patch_mlps()\\n\\n    def _patch_mlps(self):\\n        for layer_idx, layer in enumerate(self.model.model.layers):\\n            self._patch_single_mlp(layer_idx, layer.mlp)\\n\\n    def _patch_single_mlp(self, layer_idx, mlp):\\n        gate_proj = mlp.gate_proj\\n        up_proj = mlp.up_proj\\n        down_proj = mlp.down_proj\\n        act_fn = mlp.act_fn\\n        wrapper = self\\n        predictor = self.predictors[layer_idx]\\n\\n        def patched_forward(x):\\n            gate = act_fn(gate_proj(x))\\n            up = up_proj(x)\\n            intermediate = gate * up\\n\\n            if wrapper.sparse_mode:\\n                logits = predictor(x)\\n                mask = wrapper.gumbel_mask(logits)\\n                wrapper._layer_masks[layer_idx] = mask\\n                result = down_proj(intermediate * mask)\\n                if wrapper.use_compensation and wrapper.comp_network is not None:\\n                    result = result + wrapper.comp_network.forward_layer(layer_idx, x)\\n                return result\\n            elif wrapper.capture_intermediates:\\n                wrapper._layer_intermediates[layer_idx] = intermediate.detach()\\n                wrapper._layer_inputs[layer_idx] = x.detach()\\n\\n            return down_proj(intermediate)\\n\\n        mlp.forward = patched_forward\\n\\n    def forward(self, input_ids, attention_mask=None, labels=None):\\n        self._layer_masks.clear()\\n        return self.model(\\n            input_ids=input_ids,\\n without masks. Optionally captures per-layer intermediates & inputs.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.sparse_mode = False\\n        self.capture_intermediates = capture_intermediates\\n        self._layer_intermediates.clear()\\n        self._layer_inputs.clear()\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            outputs = self.model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\\n        self.sparse_mode = True\\n        self.capture_intermediates = False\\n        return outputs\\n\\n    def forward_sparse(self, input_ids, attention_mask=None, labels=None):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Forward with predicted sparsity masks.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.sparse_mode = True\\n        return self.forward(input_ids, attention_mask, labels)\\n\\n    def get_masks(self):\\n        return dict(self._layer_masks)\\n\\n    def get_intermediates(self):\\n        return dict(self._layer_intermediates)\\n\\n    def get_layer_inputs(self):\\n        return dict(self._layer_inputs)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 2.14}}"

Scaled by T^2 so gradient magnitude is\\n    independent of temperature.\\n    Normalized to per-token-per-dim scale.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, temperature: float = 1.0):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.temperature = temperature\\n\\n    def forward(self, dense_logits: torch.Tensor, sparse_logits: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        p = F.softmax(dense_logits / self.temperature, dim=-1)\\n        log_q = F.log_softmax(sparse_logits / self.temperature, dim=-1)\\n        kl = F.kl_div(log_q, p, reduction=\\\"batchmean\\\") * (self.temperature ** 2)\\n        seq_len = dense_logits.shape[-2]\\n        return kl / seq_len\\n\\n\\nclass BCESparsityLoss(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"BCE between predictor logits and top-k binary activation targets.\\n\\n    For each position, the top (1-sparsity) fraction of neurons by absolute\\n    activation value are labeled 1 (keep); the rest 0 (prune).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, sparsity_target: float = 0.5):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.sparsity_target = sparsity_target\\n\\n    def forward(self, predictor_logits: torch.Tensor, dense_intermediate: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        k = int(dense_intermediate.shape[-1] * (1 - self.sparsity_target))\\n        abs_vals = dense_intermediate.abs()\\n        topk_vals, _ = abs_vals.topk(k, dim=-1)\\n        threshold = topk_vals[..., -1:]\\n        binary_target = (abs_vals >= threshold).float()\\n        return F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(predictor_logits, binary_target)\\n\\n\\nclass CompensationLoss(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"MSE between (sparse_mlp_output + compensation) and dense_mlp_output.\\n\\n    Operates per-layer in hidden_size space (after down_proj).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def forward(self, compensation: torch.Tensor, sparse_output: torch.Tensor,\\n                dense_output: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        return F.mse_loss(sparse_output + compensation, dense_output)\\n\\n\\nclass SparsityRegularizer(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"L1 penalty driving average mask density toward (1 - target_sparsity).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, target_sparsity: float = 0.5, weight: float = 1.0):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.target_sparsity = target_sparsity\\n        self.weight = weight\\n\\n    def forward(self, masks: dict) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        if not masks:\\n            return torch.tensor(0.0)\\n        penalties = []\\n        for mask in masks.values():\\n            density = mask.mean()\\n            penalties.append(torch.abs(density - \\\"\\\"\\\"Streams text, tokenizes, and packs into fixed-length (seq_len) chunks.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, hf_dataset, tokenizer, seq_len: int, seed: int = 42):\\n        self.dataset = hf_dataset\\n        self.tokenizer = tokenizer\\n        self.seq_len = seq_len\\n        self.seed = seed\\n\\n    def __iter__(self):\\n        buffer = []\\n        for example in add_special_tokens=False)[\\\"input_ids\\\"]\\n            buffer.extend(tokens)\\n            while len(buffer) >= self.seq_len + 1:\\n                chunk = buffer[: self.seq_len + 1]\\n                buffer = buffer[self.seq_len + 1 :]\\n                input_ids = torch.tensor(chunk[:-1], dtype=torch.long)\\n                labels = torch.tensor(chunk[1:], dtype=torch.long)\\n datasets import load_from_disk\\n            ds = load_from_disk(_wt103_local)\\n        else:\\n            ds = load_dataset(\\\"wikitext\\\", \\\"wikitext-103-raw-v1\\\", split=\\\"train\\\")\\n        ds = ds.shuffle(seed=seed)\\n    elif dataset_name == \\\"c4\\\":\\n        _c4_local = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/data/c4_val\\\"\\n        if os.path.isdir(_c4_local):\\n            from datasets torch.sigmoid(pred_logits)\\n                hard_mask = (pred_logits > 0).float()\\n                all_masks[layer_idx] = hard_mask - soft_mask.detach() + soft_mask\\n\\n            total_bce = total_bce / max(num_layers, 1)\\n            reg = reg_fn(all_masks)\\n            loss = (total_bce + reg) / ga\\n            loss.backward()\\n\\n            accum_loss += loss.item()\\n Data\\n    print(\\\"Loading C4 calibration data...\\\")\\n    loader = get_calibration_loader(tokenizer, batch_size=1, seq_len=seq_len, seed=42, dataset_name=\\\"c4\\\")\\n\\n    # Accumulators: per-layer and global\\n    stats = {name: {\\\"tp\\\": torch.zeros(num_layers, device=device),\\n                    \\\"fp\\\": torch.zeros(num_layers, device=device),\\n                    \\\"fn\\\": torch.zeros(num_layers, device=device)}\\n             for name in predictors}\\n\\n    # Hook to capture layer inputs and intermediates\\n    layer_data = {}\\n\\n    def make_hook(layer_idx):\\n        def hook_fn(module, args, output):\\n            x = args[0]\\n            gate = module.act_fn(module.gate_proj(x))\\n            up = module.up_proj(x)\\n            intermediate = gate * up\\n            layer_data[layer_idx] = {\\\"input\\\": x, \\\"intermediate\\\": intermediate}\\n        return hook_fn\\n\\n    hooks = []\\n    for li, layer in enumerate(model.model.layers):\\n        h = layer.mlp.register_forward_hook(make_hook(li))\\n        hooks.append(h)\\n\\n    n_processed = 0\\n    for batch in loader:\\n        if n_processed >= num_samples:\\n            break\\n        input_ids = batch[\\\"input_ids\\\"].unsqueeze(0).to(device)\\n        layer_data.clear()\\n\\n        model(input_ids=input_ids)\\n\\n        for li in range(num_layers):\\n            if li not in layer_data:\\n                continue\\n            intermediate = layer_data[li][\\\"intermediate\\\"]  # (1, seq, intermediate_size)\\n            hidden_input = layer_data[li][\\\"input\\\"]          # (1, seq, hidden_size)\\n\\n            # Oracle: magnitude top-k\\n            mag = intermediate.abs()\\n            k = int(intermediate_size * sparsity)\\n            _, topk_idx = mag.topk(k, dim=-1)\\n            oracle_mask = torch.zeros_like(intermediate, dtype=torch.bool)\\n            oracle_mask.scatter_(-1, topk_idx, True)\\n\\n            for name, preds in predictors.items():\\n                logits = preds[li](hidden_input)\\n                pred_mask = logits > 0  # hard threshold at inference\\n\\n                tp = (pred_mask & oracle_mask).sum().item()\\n                fp = (pred_mask & ~oracle_mask).sum().item()\\n                fn = (~pred_mask & oracle_mask).sum().item()\\n\\n                stats[name][\\\"tp\\\"][li] += tp\\n                stats[name][\\\"fp\\\"][li] += fp\\n                stats[name][\\\"fn\\\"][li] += fn\\n\\n        n_processed += 1\\n        if n_processed % 10 == 0:\\n            print(f\\\"  Processed {n_processed}/{num_samples} samples\\\")\\n\\n    for h in hooks:\\n        h.remove()\\n\\n    # Compute metrics\\n    results = {}\\n    for name in predictors:\\n        tp = stats[name][\\\"tp\\\"]\\n        fp = stats[name][\\\"fp\\\"]\\n        fn = stats[name][\\\"fn\\\"]\\n\\n        precision_per_layer = tp / (tp + fp + 1e-8)\\n        recall_per_layer = tp / (tp + fn + 1e-8)\\n        f1_per_layer = 2 * precision_per_layer * recall_per_layer / (precision_per_layer + recall_per_layer + 1e-8)\\n\\n        tp_total = tp.sum().item()\\n        fp_total = fp.sum().item()\\n        fn_total = fn.sum().item()\\n\\n        precision = tp_total / (tp_total + fp_total + 1e-8)\\n        recall = tp_total / (tp_total + fn_total + 1e-8)\\n        f1 = 2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall + 1e-8)\\n\\n        results[name] = {\\n            \\\"f1\\\": round(f1, 4),\\n            \\\"precision\\\": round(precision, 4),\\n            \\\"recall\\\": round(recall, 4),\\n            \\\"per_layer_f1\\\": [round(x, 4) for x in f1_per_layer.tolist()],\\n            \\\"per_layer_precision\\\": [round(x, 4) for x in precision_per_layer.tolist()],\\n            \\\"per_layer_recall\\\": [round(x, 4) for x in recall_per_layer.tolist()],\\n        }\\n\\n    # Random baseline\\n    expected_f1_random = 2 * sparsity * sparsity / (sparsity + sparsity)  # = sparsity for 50%\\n    results[\\\"random_baseline\\\"] = {\\n        \\\"f1\\\": round(expected_f1_random, 4),\\n        \\\"precision\\\": round(sparsity, 4),\\n        \\\"recall\\\": round(sparsity, 4),\\n    }\\n\\n    output = {\\n        \\\"config\\\": {\\n            \\\"num_samples\\\": num_samples,\\n            \\\"seq_len\\\": seq_len,\\n            \\\"sparsity_target\\\": sparsity,\\n            \\\"model\\\": model_path,\\n        },\\n        **results,\\n    }\\n\\n    # Summary\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n=== F1 vs Oracle Magnitude Mask (top-50%) ===\\\")\\n    for name in list(predictors.keys()) + [\\\"random_baseline\\\"]:\\n        r = results[name]\\n        print(f\\\"  {name:20s}: F1={r['f1']:.4f}  P={r['precision']:.4f}  R={r['recall']:.4f}\\\")\\n\\n    out_path = \\\"/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/artifacts/kl_bce_f1_oracle.json\\\"\\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out_path), exist_ok=True)\\n    with open(out_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nSaved to {out_path}\\\")\\n\\n    return output\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    compute_f1_vs_oracle()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.91}}"}

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have a clear picture of:

1. **KL training flow** (`train_kl_normalized`):
   - Does full forward pass: `wrapper.forward_dense(input_ids)` for dense logits, then `wrapper.forward_sparse(input_ids)` for sparse logits
   - The gradient flows through the predictor via the Gumbel-sigmoid mask → the entire model forward pass → output logits → KL loss
   - This creates "gradient coupling" because the loss is on output logits, and the gradient must flow back through the model's own computation
   
2. **BCE training flow** (`train_bce`):
   - Captures dense activations from a forward pass without gradients, then runs the predictor on each layer's captured inputs
   - Computes BCE loss between predictor outputs and magnitude-based targets
   - Gradients only flow through the predictor itself, completely decoupling from the base model

3. **The oracle experiment**: I need to load the KL predictor checkpoint and run data through the model with hooks to capture layer inputs to get the oracle predictions.

For the key experiment, I'll use the KL predictor's soft probability outputs as targets instead of binary masks—this lets me test whether the predictor's advantage comes from gradient coupling through the full model or just from learning better decision boundaries. I'll generate soft targets from the KL predictor's logits, then train a new predictor with BCE loss against those targets while keeping the base model frozen. same hyperparams as KL training (lr=1e-3, 1000 steps, batch_size=4, AdamW)
- The loss would be F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(new_predictor_logits, soft_targets)

Actually, I need to think about this more carefully. The "soft target" from the KL predictor should be the sigmoid(logits), which represents the probability that each neuron should be kept. This is the mask probability, not the activation probability.

Wait, let me re-read the approach more carefully. The KL predictor produces logits, and during inference, `logits > 0` means "keep this neuron". So `sigmoid(logits)` gives the probability of keeping each neuron.

For the oracle experiment:
- I run data through the model
- Capture layer inputs
- Run KL predictor on those inputs → get logits
- `sigmoid(logits)` = soft probability of keeping each neuron
- Train a new predictor with BCE loss against these soft targets

This works because the new predictor learns to replicate the KL predictor's mask decisions without the gradient coupling. I'm considering two approaches: generating targets on the fly during training, or pre-computing them all upfront. The online approach seems cleaner and fits better with the existing code structure.

For the sparsity regularizer, I should keep it consistent with the original BCE training setup (target_sparsity=0.5, weight=0.1) to ensure the new predictor also learns to maintain 50% sparsity, even though the KL predictor already converges to that constraint naturally.

Now I have a complete understanding of the codebase. Let me write the `c3_coupling_oracle.py` script.

Key design decisions:
1. Load the KL predictor checkpoint as a frozen "oracle" teacher
2. Load the model with forward hooks to capture layer inputs
3. For each batch:
   - Forward through the model (no grad) to capture layer inputs
   - Run frozen KL predictor on layer inputs → sigmoid(logits) = soft targets
   - Run trainable new predictor on same layer inputs → get logits
   - Compute BCE loss between new predictor logits and KL oracle's soft targets


4. Match the KL training setup with the same learning rate, step count, batch size, and cosine warmup schedule, while applying the sparsity regularizer to maintain the target sparsity level.
```

default=\"distill_sparse_swiglu\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--wandb_run_name\", type=str, default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--warmup_steps\", type=int, default=100)\n    p.add_argument(\"--log_every\", type=int, default=100)\n    p.add_argument(\"--bottleneck_dim\", type=int, default=128)\n    p.add_argument(\"--dataset\", type=str, default=\"wikitext-103\",\n                   choices=[\"wikitext-103\", \"c4\"])\n    p.add_argument(\"--gradient_accumulation_steps\", type=int, default=1)\n    p.add_argument(\"--use_random_data\", action=\"store_true\",\n                   help=\"Use random tokens instead of real data (for dry-run)\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--dry_run_steps\", type=int, default=0,\n                   help=\"If >0, override num_steps for quick validation\")\n    return p.parse_args()\n\n\ndef main():\n    args = parse_args()\n    if args.dry_run_steps > 0:\n        args.num_steps = args.dry_run_steps\n\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\")\n\n    run_name = args.wandb_run_name or f\"c3_bce_kl_oracle_s{args.seed}\"\n    wandb.init(project=args.wandb_project, name=run_name, config=vars(args))\n\n    print(f\"Loading model from {args.model_name_or_path} ...\")\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_name_or_path)\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n        args.model_name_or_path,\n        torch_dtype=torch.float16,\n        attn_implementation=\"sdpa\",\n    ).to(device).eval()\n\n    config = model.config\n    num_layers = config.num_hidden_layers\n    hidden_size = config.hidden_size\n    intermediate_size = config.intermediate_size\n    print(f\"Model: {num_layers} layers, hidden={hidden_size}, intermediate={intermediate_size}\")\n\n    # Load frozen KL oracle predictor\n    print(f\"Loading KL oracle from {args.kl_checkpoint} ...\")\n    kl_ckpt = torch.load(args.kl_checkpoint, map_location=device, weights_only=True)\n    oracle_predictors = torch.nn.ModuleList([\n        SparsityPredictor(hidden_size, intermediate_size, args.bottleneck_dim)\n        for _ in range(num_layers)\n    ]).to(device)\n    oracle_predictors.load_state_dict(kl_ckpt[\"predictors\"])\n    oracle_predictors.half().eval()\n    for p in oracle_predictors.parameters():\n        p.requires_grad = False\n    print(\"KL oracle loaded and frozen\")\n\n    # Trainable student predictor (fresh init)\n    student_predictors = torch.nn.ModuleList([\n        SparsityPredictor(hidden_size, intermediate_size, args.bottleneck_dim)\n        for _ in range(num_layers)\n    ]).to(device)\n    student_predictors.train()\n\n    # Hook to capture MLP inputs\n    layer_inputs = {}\n\n    def make_hook(layer_idx):\n        def hook_fn(module, args_tuple, output):\n            layer_inputs[layer_idx] = args_tuple[0].detach()\n        return hook_fn\n\n    hooks = []\n    for li, layer in enumerate(model.model.layers):\n        h = layer.mlp.register_forward_hook(make_hook(li))\n        hooks.append(h)\n\n    # Data\n    if args.use_random_data:\n        dataloader = get_random_loader(config.vocab_size, args.batch_size, args.seq_len, args.seed)\n    else:\n        dataloader = get_calibration_loader(\n            tokenizer, args.batch_size, args.seq_len, args.seed, args.dataset)\n\n    # Optimizer\n    params = list(student_predictors.parameters())\n    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(params, lr=args.lr, weight_decay=0.01)\n    scheduler = cosine_schedule_with_warmup(optimizer, args.warmup_steps, args.num_steps)\n\n    ga = args.gradient_accumulation_steps\n    data_iter = iter(dataloader)\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    print(f\"\\nTraining: {args.num_steps} steps, bs={args.batch_size}, ga={ga}, \"\n          f\"lr={args.lr}, warmup={args.warmup_steps}\")\n    print(f\"Sparsity target={args.sparsity_target}, reg_weight={args.sparsity_reg_weight}\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    for opt_step in range(1, args.num_steps + 1):\n        accum_loss = 0.0\n        accum_bce = 0.0\n        accum_reg = 0.0\n        last_sparsity = 0.0\n\n        for _ in range(ga):\n            try:\n                batch = next(data_iter)\n            except StopIteration:\n                data_iter = iter(dataloader)\n                batch = next(data_iter)\n\n            input_ids = batch[\"input_ids\"].to(device)\n            layer_inputs.clear()\n\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                model(input_ids=input_ids)\n\n            total_bce = torch.tensor(0.0, device=device)\n            all_masks = {}\n\n            for li in range(num_layers):\n                if li not in layer_inputs:\n                    continue\n                inp = layer_inputs[li]  # (batch, seq, hidden_size), detached\n\n                # Oracle: KL predictor's soft probability (frozen, no grad)\n                with torch.no_grad():\n                    oracle_logits = oracle_predictors[li](inp.half())\n                    soft_target = torch.sigmoid(oracle_logits).float()\n\n                # Student: trainable predictor\n                student_logits = student_predictors[li](inp.float())\n\n                # BCE loss with soft target\n                bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(student_logits, soft_target)\n                total_bce = total_bce + bce\n\n                # Track mask for sparsity regularization\n                with torch.no_grad():\n                    hard_mask = (student_logits > 0).float()\n                    all_masks[li] = hard_mask\n\n            total_bce = total_bce / max(num_layers, 1)\n\n            # Sparsity regularizer (same as BCE baseline)\n            if all_masks:\n                density = torch.stack([(m > 0.5).float().mean() for m in all_masks.values()]).mean()\n                reg = args.sparsity_reg_weight * torch.abs(density - (1.0 - args.sparsity_target))\n            else:\n                reg = torch.tensor(0.0, device=device)\n\n            loss = (total_bce + reg) / ga\n            loss.backward()\n\n            accum_loss += loss.item()\n            accum_bce += total_bce.item() / ga\n            accum_reg += reg.item() / ga\n\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                last_sparsity = (\n                    1.0 - torch.stack([(m > 0.5).float().mean() for m in all_masks.values()]).mean().item()\n                    if all_masks else 0.0\n                )\n\n        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, 1.0)\n        optimizer.step()\n        scheduler.step()\n        optimizer.zero_grad()\n\n        elapsed = time.time() - t0\n        log_dict = {\n            \"loss\": accum_loss, \"bce_loss\": accum_bce, \"reg_loss\": accum_reg,\n            \"sparsity\": last_sparsity, \"lr\": scheduler.get_last_lr()[0],\n            \"wall_time\": elapsed, \"step_time\": elapsed / opt_step,\n        }\n        wandb.log(log_dict, step=opt_step)\n\n        if opt_step % args.log_every == 0 or opt_step == 1:\n            print(\n                f\"[Step {opt_step}/{args.num_steps}] loss={accum_loss:.4f} \"\n                f\"bce={accum_bce:.4f} reg={accum_reg:.4f} \"\n                f\"sparsity={last_sparsity:.3f} lr={scheduler.get_last_lr()[0]:.2e} \"\n                f\"elapsed={elapsed:.1f}s ({elapsed/opt_step:.2f}s/step)\"\n            )\n\n    # Cleanup hooks\n    for h in hooks:\n        h.remove()\n\n    # Save\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    ckpt_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, \"predictor_bce_kl_oracle.pt\")\n    torch.save({\n        \"predictors\": student_predictors.state_dict(),\n        \"args\": vars(args),\n        \"kl_checkpoint_used\": args.kl_checkpoint,\n    }, ckpt_path)\n    total_time = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"\\nTraining complete: {args.num_steps} steps in {total_time:.1f}s \"\n          f\"({total_time/args.num_steps:.2f}s/step)\")\n    print(f\"Saved checkpoint -> {ckpt_path}\")\n\n    pred_params = sum(p.numel() for p in student_predictors.parameters())\n    print(f\"Predictor params: {pred_params:,} ({pred_params * 4 / 1024**2:.1f} MB in fp32)\")\n\n    wandb.finish()\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nSCRIPT",
  "account": "root"
}

### Assistant

_source line 72_

Dry-run 完全成功。5 steps 正常运行，训练循环无报错。

**结果总结：**

脚本 `/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/scripts/c3_coupling_oracle.py` 已就绪。

**关键参数：**
| 参数 | 默认值 | 说明 |
|------|--------|------|
| `--num_steps` | 1000 | 与 KL 训练一致 |
| `--batch_size` | 4 | 与 KL 训练一致 |
| `--lr` | 1e-3 | 与 KL 训练一致 |
| `--bottleneck_dim` | 128 | d_b=128，与 KL/BCE 一致 |
| `--sparsity_target` | 0.5 | 50% sparsity |
| `--sparsity_reg_weight` | 0.1 | 与 BCE 训练一致 |
| `--kl_checkpoint` | `checkpoints/mvp_kl_norm_v2_s42/predictor_kl_normalized.pt` | 冻结 KL oracle |
| `--dataset` | wikitext-103 | 与 KL 训练一致 |

**预估训练资源：**
- GPU: 1× RTX PRO 6000 (`cuda:0`)，无需梯度回传基础模型
- 时间: ~0.3s/step × 1000 steps ≈ **~5 分钟**（远快于 KL 的 ~45min，因为无需 gradient checkpointing/模型反向传播）
- 显存: ~16GB（仅基础模型 fp16 推理 + predictor fp32 训练）

**正式训练命令：**
```bash
cd /root/distill_sparse_swiglu && WANDB_MODE=disabled nohup /root/miniconda3/bin/python scripts/c3_coupling_oracle.py --gpu 0 --dataset wikitext-103 > logs/c3_coupling_oracle.log 2>&1 &
```

**实验逻辑：**
- 每个 batch：基础模型 forward（no grad）→ 用冻结 KL predictor 生成 sigmoid(logits) 作为 soft target → 新 predictor 以 BCE loss 拟合这些 soft target
- 去掉了 gradient coupling（梯度不流经基础模型），保留了 KL oracle 的"什么该 prune"的信息
- 训完后对比 PPL，即可判断 hypothesis A vs B
